# References

This project would not be possible without the dedicated work of the researchers, observers, instrument teams and data archives whose papers and datasets it builds on. Every number the generator draws from physics, every law of how stars, planets, moons, belts and atmospheres come to be, was first measured, modelled, checked and written down by people who spent years, often a whole career, on a single question and then shared the answer openly so that anyone could build on it. The telescopes and spacecraft teams, the laboratory spectroscopists, the survey collaborations, the modellers and the volunteers who keep archives alive are why a made-up galaxy can still obey the same physics as the real one. We are deeply grateful to every one of them, and this page is our attempt to name the work we stand on.

## How to read this list

References follow the AAS journal and NASA Astrophysics Data System (ADS) style: author-year, alphabetical by first author and then by the later authors and the year, with the journal in its ADS abbreviation, then volume and first page (or article number). Up to five authors are named; longer author lists shorten to the first author and "et al." (the full list is on the linked page). Where one first author has more than one paper in a year, the years carry the letters a, b, c. The title is printed in italics, which the AAS journals now permit. Each entry gives the DOI, the arXiv identifier when the paper has one, and two links: the DOI or arXiv page, and the ADS abstract page. Metadata come from Crossref, DataCite and the arXiv API; nothing was filled in from memory.

This page is written by `scripts/build_references.py` from `docs/reference/references-data.json`; the HTML twin [REFERENCES.html](REFERENCES.html) is built from the same data. Do not edit either by hand.

**2438 papers and books, 6 datasets with their own DOI, 46 archives, catalogs and tools, and 2546 citations still to be settled.**

## Datasets, catalogs, archives and tools

Archives and catalogs the project reads, with the page each is served from. A dataset the setup step downloads says so; the exact files and checksums are in `src/planetgen/datasets/catalogue.py`.

- Baraffe et al. (2015) BHAC15 evolutionary models. Centre de Recherche Astrophysique de Lyon. <https://perso.ens-lyon.fr/isabelle.baraffe/BHAC15dir/>
- Baraffe et al. 2015 (BHAC15) isochrones of low-mass stars and brown dwarfs. Baraffe, Homeier, Allard and Chabrier 2015, A&A 577, A42 (downloaded at setup). <https://perso.ens-lyon.fr/>
- BurnMan, a mineral physics toolkit. Cottaar et al.; CIG Geodynamics. <https://geodynamics.github.io/burnman/>
- Bédard et al. 2020 white-dwarf cooling sequences. Bédard, Bergeron, Brassard and Fontaine 2020, ApJ 901, 93 (doi:10.3847/1538-4357/abafbe) (downloaded at setup). <https://warwick.ac.uk/>
- Cantera, chemical kinetics and thermodynamics (NASA-7 polynomials). Cantera developers. <https://cantera.org/>
- Castelli and Kurucz ATLAS9 model-atmosphere grids. Osservatorio Astronomico di Trieste. <https://wwwuser.oats.inaf.it/castelli/grids.html>
- Collision-induced absorption of N2, O2, CO2, H2, He and CH4 pairs (HITRAN CIA, 2011 to 2024 releases). Karman et al. 2019 (Icarus 328, 160), HITRAN collision-induced absorption files from hitran.org (downloaded at setup). <https://hitran.org/>
- Crystallography Open Database. Crystallography Open Database project. <https://www.crystallography.net/cod/>
- Edinburgh DataShare. University of Edinburgh. <https://datashare.ed.ac.uk/>
- ExoMol database and ExoMolOP opacities. ExoMol project, University College London. <https://exomol.com/>
- ExoPlex, a Python toolkit for exoplanet interiors. Unterborn et al.. <https://github.com/CaymanUnterborn/ExoPlex>
- Gaia archive and data releases. European Space Agency, Gaia Data Processing and Analysis Consortium. <https://gea.esac.esa.int/archive/>
- Handbook of Mineralogy. Mineral Data Publishing and the Mineralogical Society of America. <https://www.handbookofmineralogy.org/>
- Harris catalog of Milky Way globular cluster parameters (2010 edition). McMaster University. <https://physwww.mcmaster.ca/~harris/Databases.html>
- Harris catalog of parameters for Milky Way globular clusters (2010 edition). Harris 1996, AJ 112, 1487 (2010 edition), McMaster University (downloaded at setup). <https://physwww.mcmaster.ca/>
- ICRP Publication 103. International Commission on Radiological Protection. <https://www.icrp.org/publication.asp?id=ICRP+Publication+103>
- Infrared correlated-k tables of H2O, CO2 and CH4 (ExoMolOP, R1000, line lists POKAZATEL, UCL-4000 and YT34to10). Chubb et al. 2021 (A&A 646, A21), ExoMolOP R1000 ARCiS k-tables from exomol.com (downloaded at setup). <https://exomol.com/>
- JPL Small-Body Database. NASA Jet Propulsion Laboratory. <https://ssd.jpl.nasa.gov/tools/sbdb_lookup.html>
- JPL Solar System Dynamics: planetary satellite physical and mean orbital parameters. NASA Jet Propulsion Laboratory. <https://ssd.jpl.nasa.gov/sats/>
- Local Volume Database. Pace et al.. <https://github.com/apace7/local_volume_database>
- Lodders (2003) solar system abundances and condensation temperatures. The Astrophysical Journal. <https://doi.org/10.1086/375492>
- Mamajek mean dwarf stellar colour and effective temperature sequence (2022.04.16). Pecaut and Mamajek 2013, ApJS 208, 9 (doi:10.1088/0067-0049/208/1/9), version 2022.04.16 of E. Mamajek's table (downloaded at setup). <https://www.pas.rochester.edu/>
- Mean dwarf stellar color and effective temperature sequence (E. Mamajek, 2022.04.16). University of Rochester. <https://www.pas.rochester.edu/~emamajek/EEM_dwarf_UBVIJHK_colors_Teff.txt>
- MESA Isochrones and Stellar Tracks (MIST) v1.2. Harvard-Smithsonian Center for Astrophysics. <https://waps.cfa.harvard.edu/MIST/>
- MIST v1.2 isochrones, main sequence (v/vcrit 0.4, solar-scaled). Choi et al. 2016 (ApJ 823, 102) and Dotter 2016 (ApJS 222, 8), MIST v1.2, mist.science (downloaded at setup). <https://mist.science/>
- Model-atmosphere spectra: Castelli and Kurucz ATLAS9 fluxes (3500 to 50000 K) and Husser et al. PHOENIX-ACES (2300 to 3500 K). Castelli and Kurucz 2003 (IAU Symp. 210, A20), Husser et al. 2013 (A&A 553, A6) (downloaded at setup). <https://wwwuser.oats.inaf.it/>
- MPI-Mainz UV/VIS Spectral Atlas of Gaseous Molecules. Max Planck Institute for Chemistry, Mainz. <https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.6951>
- NASA Exoplanet Archive. NASA Exoplanet Science Institute, Caltech/IPAC. <https://exoplanetarchive.ipac.caltech.edu/>
- NASA Planetary Data System. NASA. <https://pds.nasa.gov/>
- Neutron-star equations of state (Malik et al. 2024) and outer-crust compositions (Servais and Chamel 2026). Malik, Pais and Providência 2024 (A&A 689, A242) and Servais and Chamel 2026 (A&A 710, A322), Zenodo (downloaded at setup). <https://zenodo.org/>
- NIST Chemistry WebBook, NIST Standard Reference Database 69. National Institute of Standards and Technology. <https://webbook.nist.gov/chemistry/>
- NIST-JANAF Thermochemical Tables. National Institute of Standards and Technology. <https://janaf.nist.gov/>
- NMSU planetary datasets (Kuiper-belt, giant-planet infrared spectra). New Mexico State University. <https://astronomy.nmsu.edu/>
- NOAA solar proton events above 10 MeV, 1976 to 2017. NOAA Space Environment Services Center, Solar Proton Events Affecting the Earth Environment, via NASA SDAC (downloaded at setup). <https://umbra.nascom.nasa.gov/>
- NOAA Space Weather Prediction Center solar proton events. NOAA SWPC. <https://www.swpc.noaa.gov/products/solar-proton-events>
- Ozone absorption cross sections, 195 to 830 nm at 218, 243 and 295 K, and the Chappuis band at 220 to 298 K (Brion, Daumont, Malicet; Burkholder and Talukdar; MPI-Mainz atlas). Keller-Rudek et al. 2013 (ESSD 5, 365), MPI-Mainz UV/VIS Spectral Atlas snapshot 2013-06-24, Zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.6951, CC BY 4.0 (downloaded at setup). <https://zenodo.org/>
- Particle Data Group, atomic and nuclear properties. Particle Data Group, Lawrence Berkeley National Laboratory. <https://pdg.lbl.gov/>
- PHOENIX-ACES stellar spectra library. Goettingen Spectral Library. <https://phoenix.astro.physik.uni-goettingen.de/>
- PubChem. National Center for Biotechnology Information. <https://pubchem.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/>
- SeaFreeze, thermodynamics of water and ice. Journaux et al.. <https://github.com/Bjournaux/SeaFreeze>
- SIMBAD astronomical database. Centre de Donnees astronomiques de Strasbourg. <https://simbad.cds.unistra.fr/>
- Three.js JavaScript 3D library. mrdoob and contributors. <https://threejs.org/>
- UNSCEAR 2008 Report, Annex B. United Nations Scientific Committee on the Effects of Atomic Radiation. <https://www.unscear.org/unscear/en/publications/2008_1.html>
- USGS Spectral Library Version 7 (splib07a). U.S. Geological Survey. <https://doi.org/10.3133/ds1035>
- VizieR catalogue access tool. Centre de Donnees astronomiques de Strasbourg. <https://vizier.cds.unistra.fr/>
- Webmineral Mineralogy Database. David Barthelmy. <http://webmineral.com/>
